# Review — 066-audit-records-and-the-completion-gate

## Summary

The window runs from 066's last review (`945217ff`) to HEAD. Two agents ran all five passes, each over half the window's commits, and each read all nine loaded backend and cross rule files whole (the two changed by `5d3a8be2` and `47844231` mid-run judged at their new text): half A the audit records, waivers, decisions and gate (`a3beb4c0`, `58d4311b`, `80b43a11`, `fdc6d3c1`, `8f9ab6d6`, `9a6a5ab3`, `6a08a7ad`, `5a746e34`), half B the review scope and Family 19 (`65ec2d7f`, `008338d6`, `ecbd316a`, `94e5b151`, `02bda11a`, `53d3d720`, `481017da`, `3161ccb8`, and the no-spec hunks of `2604a6f9` and `b1d319f1`). Each read its commits' hunks, most with the functions they touch; two hunks went unread: `65ec2d7f`'s in `runtime/tests/golden/implement-basic.jsonl` and `6a08a7ad`'s test in `check_review_gate.rs`, and half B read `interpreter/mod.rs`, `tests/mcp.rs` and `main.rs` at their hunks alone. Of the 114 scope entries 43 were read whole: 066's spec, plan, tasks, analysis and data model, its scenarios in scope (all 25 were read as contract), AGENTS.md and the constitution, and the smaller files and mutation lists the commits wrote. The rest are unread: the large runtime and test files, the release workflow, the bootstrap, the references and CHANGELOG were read at the window's hunks and what each check needed, not whole, and half B's passes did not run over the entries it left unread; 026's `spec.md`, an uncommitted edit of this run, joins the scope unread; mirrors were checked against their sources and govern.md byte-identical to ductus.md; and entries carried only by commits other specs' committed reviews read (051, 062, 071, 048, 052, 063, 026, 081, 000) were not opened. No loaded rule fired. Fixed in the run: a broken contract, process-waivers' retained notice printing a stored path raw; compute-review-scope's `deleted` counting any metadata error as a deletion; the release audit job's depth-1 checkout, on which Family 10's check 10d reports a finding (shown in a `--depth 1` clone of HEAD, where `migration-coverage.sh` exits 1); implement step 14's notice wording; 066's plan's retired Family 19 decision and stale counts, with the two citations of it re-pointed; and stale comments in registry.rs and write_review.rs. Fix round 2 re-read the implement-basic golden, re-blessed because implement step 14's edit moved its fixture repo's commit shas. The four stored decisions still standing are carried.

2 fix round(s): 2 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## Observations

- bug — process-waivers' retained notice interpolates the unflattened path through RunScope::why_unread ("this run did not read `{path}`"), so a stored waiver whose file holds a control or line-separator character, naming a file that exists on disk outside the scope, reaches the operator raw, against 066's scenario process-waivers-notices-flatten-stored-text ("no control character in a stored waiver reaches a notice"). The notices_flatten test reaches only the restricted-run retained branch, never the why_unread branch. — `runtime/src/primitives/process_waivers.rs:162-168` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — framework/commands/implement.md step 14 says a passing gate names "either record whose freshness it could not determine", but since 081 retired the review-freshness check passing_notices names only the analysis record's freshness (plus a window that could not be read and a review record without the fix-round fields); a3beb4c0 added the fix-rounds clause to this sentence. — `framework/commands/implement.md:87` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — fdc6d3c1 edited 058's data-model.md (the process-decisions result rows) while 058 is `done`; a data-model edit reopens a done spec under constitution §spec-lifecycle (classify-spec-edit reason data-model), yet 058 stays `done` and 066's spec.md declares no cross-spec-impact for it. - convention — AGENTS.md 2604a6f9's two new bullets (a docs/ commit naming no spec; a subagent's refused Write) are true for every project running the pipeline, so §drift-prevention routes them to the constitution, and AGENTS.md sits 233 bytes under its 50,000-byte ceiling — `AGENTS.md:86` — **waived**: AGENTS.md already carries sibling per-incident bullets of the same kind (the cut-off hand-back and killed-background-agent bullets) and the owner chose this home; the pre-commit hook enforces the ceiling (decided by the rule)
- bug — runtime-release.yml's `audit` job checks out at depth 1 and its comment says every step passes there, but the job runs scripts/audit/run-all.sh, whose Family 10 check 10d (migration-coverage.sh, added by 100d78c2 at 20:48 on 2026-11-11, after 2496b7c4 set depth 1 at 17:10) reports a shallow checkout as a finding, so the release audit gate fails on every ductus-v tag; framework-checks.yml keeps fetch-depth: 0 for exactly this — `.github/workflows/runtime-release.yml:46` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — registry.rs's CLI_ONLY_PRIMITIVES doc comment still says 022 task 18 'is to keep' run-generator's CLI and exec forms out of the seed allow 'until it is applied', which task 18 did on 2026-11-12 — `runtime/src/schema/registry.rs:46` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- perf — SweepIndex::explained_by re-sorts the repo-wide pair set on every call, once per file-local pair per path, instead of once at build — `runtime/src/primitives/mechanical_sweep.rs:237` — **waived**: the repo-wide set is small and the window is capped at RENAME_LIMIT paths; no measured cost, and moving the sort changes no verdict (decided by the rule)
- other — write_review.rs's doc comment and a test comment still said audit Family 31 cannot see a false empty-scope record and already reports its shape as examined-nothing, though 057 retired Family 31 (found by 020's pre-done verification, in a file 066's window holds) — `runtime/src/primitives/write_review.rs` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other: review's description and help row say it blocks done on MUST violations, while the gate also blocks an unwaived SHOULD — `framework/commands/review.md` — **waived**: true of the review record, whose blocking flag is MUST-only, and 023's AC25 pins the exact text
- other: the implement reference's "non-blocking" counts a SHOULD as blocking where review vocabulary does not — `framework/reference/implement.md` — **waived**: the inline definition names exactly which findings it covers, and non-blocking is the scenario's own term
- edge: an expired waiver on a low-confidence finding re-counts toward low-confidence, not its severity's count — `framework/reference/review.md` — **waived**: contrived: waiving a finding that counts toward no gate has no effect worth documenting, and step 1's exclusion stays true
- other: the constitution line naming plan.md carries § references resolve-anchor classes as qualified and does not judge — `framework/constitution.md` — **waived**: both targets, §brownfield-inbox and §drift-prevention, carry markers (checked by hand), and citing a subsection's marked section is the constitution's own anchor rule
